onClick={()=>setExportOpen(false)}>Back to your beat</button></div>}</Modal>}</main>;\\n# Encore — music from everyday sounds\\n\\nA mobile-friendly, **UI-only React + TypeScript application**. Collect short sounds, configure a two-bar pattern, and experiment with a step sequencer. The workspace uses flat stacked rows, charcoal and concrete grey surfaces, muted amber accents, and locally bundled fonts. It is designed for people with little or no music experience.\\n\\n## Run locally\\n\\nUse Node.js 22.12+ and npm.\\n\\n```sh\\nnpm ci\\nnpm run dev\\n```\\n\\nOpen the printed localhost URL (normally http://127.0.0.1:5173). Click **Continue with Google** to enter the demo; no account or credentials are needed.\\n\\n```sh\\nnpm run typecheck\\nnpm run build\\n```\\n\\n`npm run preview` serves the production build. Vite is only a local development/static preview server, not an application backend. No environment variables, API keys, hosted services, or server functions are required. Fonts ship with the app.\\n\\n## What works\\n\\n- Browser-local demo session and editable profile, including photo upload.\\n- Private library and seeded community library, search, sample playback, selection, visibility demonstrations, and reports.\\n- Real microphone recording via MediaRecorder; capture stops after 1.5 seconds and decoded overrun is trimmed.\\n- IndexedDB stores sample blobs and metadata. Local storage retains the demo session, profile, parameters, and beat draft.\\n- Fermata conditioning controls, deterministic local pattern generation, two-bar grids, step toggles, velocity, lane volume/mute, editable beat names, and draft restoration.\\n\\nAll samples, including seeds, pass through the shared 1.5-second import boundary. Imported clips are downmixed to mono PCM WAV. Seed audio is synthesized locally to resemble short everyday percussive/tonal sounds; it is not sourced from real community members.\\n\\n## Explicit simulations\\n\\nGoogle authentication, public sharing, report submission, model generation, beat playback, and MP3 export are **simulated**. Individual sample previews play real audio. The beat transport only animates a playhead, and export never creates or downloads a fake MP3. Profile email is a fictional demo address. Password/account-help links open Google's own pages.\\n\\n“Public” and “private” are local UI states, not access-control guarantees. Nothing is synchronized or transmitted to a backend. Browser site-data clearing removes the local workspace. Microphone capture requires localhost or HTTPS and browser permission; format support depends on browser codecs. Audio file uploads are not included.\\n\\n## Integration boundaries\\n\\nSee [docs/integration.md](docs/integration.md). Components use the typed `Services` interface in `src/services.ts`. Replace adapters there to connect a future backend; recording, trimming, and preview remain browser-side. `src/model.ts` owns domain types and pattern rules; `src/audio.ts` owns duration enforcement and WAV encoding.\\n\\n## Testing policy\\n\\nNo tests or test tooling are included. Tests will be written by hand by the team. CI only installs dependencies and builds the app.\\n\\n## Team contribution rules\\n\\n- Main is protected; use a feature branch and a pull request.\\n- Obtain at least one teammate approval and passing CI before merging; keep the branch up to date.\\n- Mention the Jira issue title in the PR description.\\n- For AI-assisted PRs, add a log under `ai_logs/` using `ai_logs/example.MD`: full chat history, tool/model, available usage/cost information, and **takeaways written manually by the contributor**. Do not invent unavailable usage data or have AI write the personal reflection.\\n- Review teammates’ PRs within a day or so.\\n\"}"
  }

The interface uses flat stacked rows, charcoal surfaces, and muted amber accents. All samples, selections, parameters, profiles, and sequencer steps are static presentation fixtures.\n\n## Run locally\n\nUse Node.js 22.12+ and npm.\n\n```sh\nnpm ci\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the printed localhost URL. The sign-in button only links to the library; no authentication takes place. Each route can also be opened directly:\n\n- `/login`\n- `/library`\n- `/parameters`\n- `/beat`\n- `/profile`\n\n`npm run build` produces a static build; `npm run preview` serves it. No API keys or backend configuration are required. Fonts are bundled locally.\n\n## Included interactions\n\nScreen navigation, library tabs, disclosure panels, info tooltips, opening/closing placeholder dialogs, and responsive sequencer wrapping. Continue buttons only navigate between screens.\n\n## Not implemented\n\nThere is no microphone access, audio capture, audio decoding, playback, waveform analysis, file upload, profile editing, search processing, sound selection, sharing, deletion, moderation, authentication, beat generation, step editing, transport timer, or export. Operational controls are disabled. Opening a recording, deletion, report, or export dialog only previews its layout and never completes an action.\n\nThere are no services, local-storage calls, IndexedDB, audio blobs, permission prompts, account sessions, or network integrations. Prior local data from earlier iterations is not read, modified, or cleared by this prototype.\n\n## Building functionality separately\n\n`src/fixtures.ts` contains static presentation data. `src/model.ts` contains presentation types and select-menu options. `src/App.tsx` contains screen components and navigation. See [docs/integration.md](docs/integration.md) for future integration boundaries. The previous audio and mock-service implementations have been removed rather than left dormant.\n\n## Testing policy\n\nNo tests or test tooling are included. Tests will be written by hand by the team. CI only installs dependencies and builds the app.\n\n'''+team)\nPath('docs/integration.md').write_text('''# Navigation prototype and future integrations\n\nEncore currently contains only presentation and navigation. It does not expose a working service layer. There are no audio, persistence, or simulated-generation implementations to reuse accidentally.\n\n## Current boundaries\n\n- `src/fixtures.ts`: named static samples, illustrated waveforms, selected-sound examples, and a fixed example pattern. Waveform numbers are decorative; samples contain no files or audio blobs.\n- `src/model.ts`: `SoundView` and `LaneView` presentation types plus parameter-menu options. No validation, generation, or storage behavior.\n- `src/App.tsx`: screen components, links, library-tab selection, accessible dialogs/tooltips, and responsive layout measurement. All screens are reachable without signing in or creating data.\n- CSS files: visual layouts and styles. Disabled controls preserve the intended design while remaining inoperative.\n\n## Future functionality to build independently\n\n| Screen | Future responsibilities |\n| --- | --- |\n| Login | Authentication, sessions, account recovery |\n| Library | Microphone capture, 1.5-second validation, playable sound assets, search, selection, ownership, visibility, deletion, reporting |\n| Parameters | User-controlled values, 60–200 BPM validation, generation request |\n| Beat editor | Real event state, edit actions, audio scheduling, volume/mute, MP3 encoding |\n| Profile | User data, photo handling, profile updates, account settings |\n\nIntroduce those capabilities explicitly through future component props or injected service interfaces. Replace fixture imports with application state only when implementing each feature. Enable controls only when their real behavior exists; do not interpret navigation to the beat screen as successful generation or the login link as authentication.\n\nFermata's conditioning vocabulary is represented as select-menu options only. The fixed pattern is an illustration, not model output. There is no model execution, MIDI/audio conversion, or sound classification.\n\nEarlier versions of the app may have left browser-local records on the same origin. This navigation prototype does not access or erase them.\n''')\nPY\ncat >> src/cartoon.css <<'EOF'\n/* Inactive capability controls retain their visual layout in this navigation prototype. */\n.preview-button:disabled,.transport-play:disabled,.step:disabled,.add-button:disabled,.icon-button:disabled,.record-circle:disabled { opacity:1; cursor:default; }\n.step:disabled:hover { background:#42423a; }\n.step.beat-start:disabled:hover { background:#515046; }\n.step.on:disabled:hover { background:#b89454; }\n.preview-button:disabled:hover { background:#3c3b34; transform:none; }\n.transport-play:disabled:hover,.record-circle:disabled:hover { background:#c1a063; color:#1b1b19; }\ninput[type=range]:disabled,select:disabled { cursor:default; opacity:.75; }\ninput[readonly] { cursor:default; }\nEOF","max_output_tokens":1000}));

Set(['visibility-private','visibility-public']);\nfunction collect(node){\n node.text.split(/\\s+/))if(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(token))classes.add(token);\n  }\n  ts.forEachChild(node,collect);\n}\nfunction files){const text=fs.readFileSync(file,'utf8');beforeBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(text);fs.writeFileSync('/tmp/encore-before-'+file.split('/').pop(),text);root.append(postcss.parse(text,{from:file}).nodes);}\nlet deadSelectors=0,overriddenDeclarations=0;\nconst removedClasses=new Set();\nroot.walkComments(node=>node.remove());\nroot.walkRules(rule=>{\n  if(rule.parent.type==='atrule'&&rule.parent.name.includes('keyframes'))return;\n  const selectors=postcss.list.comma(rule.selector);\n  const kept=selectors.filter(selector=>{\n    const required=[...selector.replace(/:not\\([^)]*\\)/g,'').matchAll(/\\.([a-zA-Z_-][\\w-]*)/g)].map(m=>m[1]);\n    const missing=required.filter(c=>!classes.has(c));\n    if(missing.length){deadSelectors++;missing.forEach(c=>removedClasses.add(c));return false;}\n    return true;\n  });\n  for(const selector of kept)rule.cloneBefore({selector});\n  rule.remove();\n});\nconst seen=new Map();\nconst rules=[];root.walkRules(r=>rules.push(r));\nfor(const rule of rules.reverse()){\n  const scope=[];for(let p=rule.parent;p&&p.type!=='root';p=p.parent)scope.unshift('@'+p.name+' '+p.params);\n  const prefix=scope.join('|')+'|'+rule.selector+'|';\n  for(const decl of [...rule.nodes].reverse()){\n    if(decl.type!=='decl')continue;\n    const key=prefix+decl.prop;\n    const later=seen.get(key);\n    if(later!==undefined&&(later||!decl.important)){decl.remove();overriddenDeclarations++;}\n    else seen.set(key,Boolean(decl.important));\n  }\n}\nfunction prune(node){for(const child of [...(node.nodes??[])]){prune(child);if(child.nodes&&!child.nodes.length)child.remove();}}\nprune(root);\nconst animations=new Set();root.walkDecls(/^animation(?:-name)?$/,d=>{for(const word of d.value.split(/[\\s,]+/))animations.add(word);});\nlet keyframesRemoved=0;root.walkAtRules(/keyframes$/,r=>{if(!animations.has(r.params)){r.remove();keyframesRemoved++;}});\n// Adjacent identical selectors can be combined without moving any declaration.\nfunction combine(parent){for(let i=0;i<(parent.nodes?.length??0);i++){const n=parent.nodes[i];if(n.type==='atrule')combine(n);const next=parent.nodes[i+1];if(n.type==='rule'&&next?.type==='rule'&&n.selector===next.selector){n.append(next.nodes);next.remove();i--;}}}\ncombine(root);\nfunction emit(node,depth=0){const pad='  '.repeat(depth);if(node.type==='decl')return `${pad}${node.prop}: ${node.value}${node.important?' !important':''};\\n`;if(node.type==='rule'||node.type==='atrule'){const head=node.type==='rule'?node.selector:'@'+node.name+(node.params?' '+node.params:'');return `${pad}${head} {\\n${node.nodes.map(n=>emit(n,depth+1)).join('')}${pad}}\\n\\n`;}return '';}\nconst result='/* Encore UI styles. Rule order preserves the existing responsive cascade. */\\n\\n'+root.nodes.map(n=>emit(n)).join('');\nfs.writeFileSync('src/styles.css',result);\nfs.unlinkSync('src/cartoon.css');fs.unlinkSync('src/bright.css');\nconst report={beforeBytes,afterBytes:Buffer.byteLength(result),deadSelectors,overriddenDeclarations,keyframesRemoved,removedClasses:[...removedClasses].sort()};\nfs.writeFileSync('/tmp/encore-cleanup-report.json',JSON.stringify(report,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(report));\nEOF\nnode /tmp/encore-css-cleanup.mjs","max_output_tokens":2200}));

defaultValue=\\\"alex.morgan@example.com\\\"/></label>\\n      <div className=\\\"account-help\\\"><LockKeyhole size={20}/><div><strong>Password & account security</strong><p>Manage your password through Google.</p></div></div>\\n      <button className=\\\"button primary\\\">Save changes</button>\\n    </div><div className=\\\"profile-signout\\\"><Link className=\\\"button secondary\\\" to=\\\"/login\\\"><LogOut size={16}/>Sign out</Link></div>\\n  </section></main>;\\n}\\n# Navigation prototype and future integrations\\n\\nEncore currently contains only presentation and navigation. It does not expose a working service layer. There are no audio, persistence, or simulated-generation implementations to reuse accidentally.\\n\\n## Current boundaries\\n\\n- `src/fixtures.ts`: named static samples, illustrated waveforms, selected-sound examples, and a fixed example pattern. Waveform numbers are decorative; samples contain no files or audio blobs.\\n- `src/model.ts`: `SoundView` and `LaneView` presentation types plus parameter-menu options. No validation, generation, or storage behavior.\\n- `src/App.tsx`: screen components, links, library-tab selection, accessible dialogs/tooltips, and responsive layout measurement. All screens are reachable without signing in or creating data.\\n- CSS files: visual layouts and styles. Controls use normal active styling. Form values may change within a screen, while application operations remain unimplemented.\\n\\n## Future functionality to build independently\\n\\n| Screen | Future responsibilities |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| Login | Authentication, sessions, account recovery |\\n| Library | Microphone capture, 1.5-second validation, playable sound assets, search, selection, ownership, visibility, deletion, reporting |\\n| Parameters | User-controlled values, 60–200 BPM validation, generation request |\\n| Beat editor | Real event state, edit actions, audio scheduling, volume/mute, MP3 encoding |\\n| Profile | User data, photo handling, profile updates, account settings |\\n\\nIntroduce those capabilities explicitly through future component props or injected service interfaces. Replace fixture imports with application state only when implementing each feature. Wire the visible controls to real behavior when ready; do not interpret navigation to the beat screen as successful generation or the login link as authentication.\\n\\nFermata's conditioning vocabulary is represented as select-menu options only. The fixed pattern is an illustration, not model output. There is no model execution, MIDI/audio conversion, or sound classification.\\n\\nEarlier versions of the app may have left browser-local records on the same origin. This navigation prototype does not access or erase them.\\n# Encore — navigation-only frontend\\n\\nReact + TypeScript screens for navigating a sound-to-beat workflow. The interface uses flat stacked rows, light surfaces, violet private sounds, teal public sounds, and blue, coral, and green accents. All samples, selections, parameters, profiles, and sequencer steps are static presentation fixtures.\\n\\n## Run locally\\n\\nUse Node.js 22.12+ and npm.\\n\\n```sh\\nnpm ci\\nnpm run dev\\n```\\n\\nOpen the printed localhost URL. The sign-in button only links to the library; no authentication takes place. Each route can also be opened directly:\\n\\n- `/login`\\n- `/library`\\n- `/parameters`\\n- `/beat`\\n- `/profile`\\n\\n`npm run build` produces a static build; `npm run preview` serves it. No API keys or backend configuration are required. Fonts are bundled locally.\\n\\n## Included interactions\\n\\nScreen navigation, transient library selection (for visual added/unadded states only), library tabs, disclosure panels, info tooltips, opening/closing placeholder dialogs, and responsive sequencer wrapping. Continue buttons only navigate between screens.\\n\\n## Not implemented\\n\\nThere is no microphone access, audio capture, audio decoding, playback, waveform analysis, file upload, profile persistence, search processing, persistent sound selection, sharing, deletion, moderation, authentication, beat generation, step editing, transport timer, or export. Controls have active styling and editable form values, but capability buttons are unconnected. Opening a recording, deletion, report, or export dialog only previews its layout and never performs an underlying operation. Confirm buttons dismiss the dialog without deleting data or creating a file.\\n\\nThere are no services, local-storage calls, IndexedDB, audio blobs, permission prompts, account sessions, or network integrations. Prior local data from earlier iterations is not read, modified, or cleared by this prototype.\\n\\n## Building functionality separately\\n\\n`src/fixtures.ts` contains static presentation data. `src/model.ts` contains presentation types and select-menu options. `src/App.tsx` contains screen components and navigation. See [docs/integration.md](docs/integration.md) for future integration boundaries. The previous audio and mock-service implementations have been removed rather than left dormant.\\n\\n## Testing policy\\n\\nNo tests or test tooling are included. Tests will be written by hand by the team. CI only installs dependencies and builds the app.\\n\\n## Team contribution rules\\n\\n- Main is protected; use a feature branch and a pull request.\\n- Obtain at least one teammate approval and passing CI before merging; keep the branch up to date.\\n- Mention the Jira issue title in the PR description.\\n- For AI-assisted PRs, add a log under `ai_logs/` using `ai_logs/example.MD`: full chat history, tool/model, available usage/cost information, and **takeaways written manually by the contributor**. Do not invent unavailable usage data or have AI write the personal reflection.\\n- Review teammates’ PRs within a day or so.\\n\\n## Typography\\n\\nFont families are defined once in `src/styles.css`. `src/main.tsx` bundles Nunito at weights 400, 500, 600, 700, and 800 and Fredoka at 500 and 600. Numeric accents use a system monospace stack and need no downloaded font. Keep new stylesheet weights aligned with these imports.\\n\"}"
  }
]
